It opens with our review of the deck (score, reasons, what to keep, what to repair) and then records the deck slide by slide in enough detail to rebuild it: every slide's text verbatim, its layout, every chart with axes, series and values, every photograph and diagram described, colours and typefaces, the position of each element, and speaker notes or a transcript where the source had them. ## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a recognizable identity (blue and orange, consistent footer, repeated deep-dive template), and many slides such as 12, 20, 24 and 30 communicate a clear point. The route is weak, though. The deck jumps from the 2026 strategy to the team and RDC, then to the market, a micro fund and four company deep dives, so the case for the fund as a whole is hard to follow. Several key slides are hard to read: the NAV table on slide 7 is tiny with dense footnotes, slide 5 has uneven columns with a very long fifth column, slide 6 states a 20% cut that the table does not show clearly, and slides 10, 21, 28, 34 and 40 are near-empty dividers that add length to a 50-page deck.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the orange and blue palette, the Vision, Product, Partners, Investment and Additional Info template on slides 24, 36, 41 and 46, and the market-number tiles on slide 20.

**Most useful repair:** Rebuild slide 7 so the headline NAV figure is large and the table is legible, redraw slide 6 as a simple before-and-after chart, and set the narrative route so that strategy, then portfolio, then deep dives is explicit.

**What would change the score:** Cut or merge the divider slides and several deep-dive pages, and fix the small text on slides 30, 31, 32 and 29 so that investors can read the portfolio at a glance.

_Confidence medium. 50 of 50 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a legal disclaimer covering forward-looking statements and the unaudited nature of the data, then a corporate overview of Elron Ventures as Israel's first evergreen fund, founded in 1961 and partnered with Rafael for over three decades. It moves into the 2026 strategy, a 2025 year-in-review, a G&A expense reduction chart, a net asset value snapshot, and the management and joint team with Rafael. Next come the defense-tech and deep-tech portfolio, the RDC partnership and new M&A-led strategy, and a market sizing section on defense spending and VC activity. The middle of the deck covers the CyberFuture micro fund and a series of company deep dives, including Raven, OpenLegacy, Tamnoon and CyberRidge, each with a problem, product, market and status arrangement. It closes with a CartiHeal update on the earn-out and a thank-you slide with contacts. Slides worth studying include the portfolio overview grid on slides 30 and 31, the Raven status slide, and the deep-dive template repeated across slides 36, 41 and 46.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Elron Ventures investor presentation have?**

The Elron Ventures investor deck has 50 pages. It opens with a disclaimer on slide 2, followed by a corporate overview on slide 3.

**What does the Elron Ventures deck say about the defense tech market size?**

Slide 20 is titled 'A $250B Opportunity' and reports VC investments in defense tech at $49.9B in 2025 vs. $37.3B in 2024. Slide 19 states that global 2026 military budgets (145 countries) total $2.86T.

**What is the Elron Ventures M&A-led strategy according to the deck?**

Slide 4 says Elron is pursuing a new M&A-led strategy through RDC with its long-standing partner Rafael, expanding from investor to strategic acquirer by acquiring and scaling early-stage defense tech companies. Slide 15 repeats this goal of acquiring and scaling early-stage defense technology companies.

**What G&A expense results does the Elron Ventures deck report?**

Slide 6 is titled 'Execution of a G&A Expense Reduction Strategy: ~20% Decrease' and shows total G&A of $5.9M in 2023, $5.8M in 2024, and $5.2M in 2025.

### Slide 4

**Text**

Elron Ventures
Elron
2026
Year-to-Date
Investments, Exits
and Strategy
M&A-Led Strategy
We are pursuing a new M&A-led strategy
through RDC with our long-standing
partner Rafael: Expanding from investor to
strategic acquirer, by acquiring and scaling
early-stage defense tech companies.*
Exit Strategy
We estimate that in the next 12
months we shall execute 1-3 exit
transactions in our portfolio
companies, including RDC's portfolio
(including by secondary transactions).
New Cybersecurity Investment – Raven
Co-investing alongside CyberFuture, leveraging our micro-fund access to participate
in high-quality cybersecurity opportunities.
Investment Strategy Focus
We continue to focus on our investment strategy in domains where our expertise,
together with our longstanding partnership Rafael, meets market urgency: deep tech,
defense tech, cybersecurity, software/AI.
The information set forth above includes "forward-looking information" as defined in Section 32A of the Israeli Securities Law, 5728-1968, and is based on the Company's assessments and
subjective assumptions as of the date of publication of the information, on information available to the Company at that time and on external information.
The information fulfilment is uncertain for reasons that depends in the company's considerations and reasons beyond the Company's control, including changes in macroeconomic
conditions and market conditions, regulatory and legal developments, financing conditions and capital market conditions, competitive and technological changes, dependence on third
parties, delays or failure in the execution of plans/transactions, the materialization of operational risks, and unforeseen events (including force majeure events).
ELRONVENTURES.COM
* The strategy has been approved by the Board of Directors of Elron and the Board of Directors of RDC, and it is also subject to approval by the Board of Directors of Rafael, as well as obtaining
the required regulatory approvals from Rafael.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white page. A left column (about x 4 to 30%) holds the logo at the top, the large title 'Elron 2026' with an orange rule beneath, and the grey subtitle, left-aligned. A right column from about x 28 to 91% holds a two-column top row (M&A-Led Strategy left, Exit Strategy right) followed by full-width sections separated by thin blue rules, all left-aligned. The disclaimer sits full width near the bottom above a thin grey rule, with the footer line beneath it. The title is not in a header band; there is no slide number visible; margins are about 4% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Elron Ventures logo at top left: a stylised mark of three stacked horizontal rounded bars in blue #508ECA, with the wordmark 'Elron / Ventures' beside it in the same blue. Short solid orange #FF6600 accent bar under the '2026' title, about x 5.3 to 12.6%, y 50.2%, about 0.9% tall, square ends. Two thin blue #508ECA horizontal divider lines (about 1 px) spanning x 27.7 to 91.3%: one at y 38.9% between the top row and the Raven section, one at y 55.5% between the Raven section and Investment Strategy Focus. One thin grey #595959 horizontal rule at y 92.4% spanning x 4.1 to 95.9% above the footer. No photographs, charts, tables or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Titles and section headings in black #000000 bold Arial (sans-serif) at about 2.96% of slide height. Body text in black #000000, regular weight, a geometric sans similar to Figtree, about 2.96% of slide height. Large title 'Elron 2026' in black #000000, regular weight, about 11.85% of slide height, with tight line spacing. Subtitle in grey #595959, regular weight, about 3.45% of slide height. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners on shapes.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A year-in-review built as a grid of metric cards (exits, dividends, investments) with a single strategy sentence on top, which makes the year easy to scan.
- Review: Several key slides are hard to read: the NAV table on slide 7 is tiny with dense footnotes, slide 5 has uneven columns with a very long fifth column, slide 6 states a 20% cut that the table does not show clearly, and slides 10, 21, 28, 34 and 40 are near-empty dividers that add length to a 50-page deck.

**Text**

2025 Summary - A Year of Building, Exits and Growth
Delivering on our strategy published in January 2025
Under new management, Elron is implementing a strategy focused on early-stage investments in defense-tech and deep-tech, alongside cyber investments. Concluding the year with exits alongside dividend distributions and share buybacks.
Strategy
Focus on defense-tech and deep-tech, alongside investments in cyber
Investments in early growth
Adoption of first dividend distribution policy1
Strengthening long-standing partnership with Rafael through RDC
Involved and active management in supporting the portfolio companies
New Investments
2 new deep-tech and defense-tech investments for a total of ~$7M2, as well as an additional investment by CyberFuture in Zeroport
Follow-on Investments
7 follow-on investments totaling ~$7M (including one follow-on investment by CyberFuture)2
Exits
3 exits valued at over $0.6B; ~$40M exit proceeds3
Dividends and Share Buyback
~$15M distributed
Strengthening our connection with the ecosystem and expanding our deal flow
Participated in and organized key events: CyberTech, DeepTech Week, Cyber Defense Week, Journey Week and the Capital Market Conference, including a joint flagship event with Rafael, alongside vertical investment content events with technology leaders, entrepreneurs and VC funds in Israel and the US.
ELRONVENTURES.COM
(1) For further details see section 5.2 in Part I of the Annual Report of 2025.
(2) Including amounts invested by RDC.
(3) Including exits of CyberFuture. Also includes Axonius shares, at a value determined in Axonius's most recent fundraising round. The economic value of the shares received may have changed since that fundraising round.
Investor Deck //
5

**Layout**

Five-column layout beneath a full-width title block. The title sits top-left at about 4% from the top and 2% from the left edge, with the grey subtitle directly beneath it. A full-width orange banner spans the slide at about 22% to 29% height, with the columns starting below it at about 31% and divided by vertical rules. The footer has a thin grey rule at about 92.5% height, with ELRONVENTURES.COM at the left, footnotes beside it, and 'Investor Deck //' with page number 5 at the right.

**Visuals**

No photographs, charts, tables, logos or icons. Non-text visuals: (a) a solid orange #FA6400 banner shape across the top of the columns, with a rounded top-right corner and square top-left corner, carrying white centred text; (b) five vertical column rules, three blue (#3F8FE0) at about x 2.2%, 20.5% and 39.8% of slide width, and two orange (#FA6400) at about x 58.7% and 78.0%, each running from about 29.3% to 80.7% of slide height and 0.1% thick; (c) a thin grey (#595959) horizontal footer rule at about y 92.5% from x 4.2% to 95.8%. Superscript footnote markers 1, 2 and 3 appear in the text.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title black #000000 in a regular geometric sans-serif (similar to Figtree), about 6.7% of slide height cap-to-line, left-aligned. Subtitle grey #595959 regular sans, about 3.5% of slide height. Column headings black #000000 in bold sans, about 3.7% of slide height. Body text black #000000 regular sans, about 2.4% of slide height; key figures such as 2, 7, 3, ~$7M, $0.6B, ~$40M and ~$15M are set slightly larger, about 3.0%. Banner text white #FFFFFF regular sans, about 2.6%. Accent colours: orange #FA6400 for banner and two rules, blue #3F8FE0 for three rules. Footer text: ELRONVENTURES.COM and Investor Deck // in black regular sans, about 1.7%; footnotes grey #595959, about 1.5%. Shapes are flat with square corners except the banner's rounded top-right corner; no shadows or gradients.

Execution of a G&A Expense Reduction
Strategy:  ~20% Decrease
The decline in G&A expenses was mainly due to
a decrease in salary expenses and efficiency
measures. As part of these measures, the
company's compensation policy was updated,
as approved by the AGM in January 2025.
It should be noted that the decrease in
expenses this year was recorded despite the
effect of the change in the exchange rate of the
dollar against the shekel, which offset part of
the decrease, so that without this effect the
actual decrease would have been higher.
($M)

Elron Snapshot
Effective and consolidated Net Asset Value
Holdings in companies / Holdings based on valuation of last financing round 1,2 ($m, effective) / Holdings based on valuation of last financing round 1,2 ($m, consolidated)
Cybersecurity/ Software 3 / 45.8 / 75.3
Deep Tech / Defense Tech / 7.8 / 10.2
Medical Device 4 / 18.7 / 18.7
Total Holdings / 72.3 / 104.2
Contingent consideration from the CartiHeal deal5 / 22.6 / 22.6
Cash & Other Financial Resources :
Other financial assets,net6 / 1.2 / 2.2
Liquid resources (as of March 16, 2026) / 42.5 / 54.9
Total Cash & Other Financial Resources : / 43.7 / 57.1
Total / 138.6 / 183.9
1 Includes Elron Ventures’ direct holdings and its effective indirect holdings through RDC. Includes investments made / after December 31, 2025. Effective Holdings Includes Elron Ventures’ balance and 50.1% of RDC’s balance. / Consolidated Holdings Includes Elron Ventures’ balance and 100% of RDC’s balance. / 2 Our holdings based on valuation of last financing round was calculated based on the “post-money” valuation of the last / financing round in the companies multiplied by our fully diluted holding percentage. It is clarified that these are / financing rounds that were carried out in different companies at different dates, and since which, in some / cases, significant time has passed. In some of the financing rounds Elron Ventures opted not to participate. / It should be noted that, even where valuations are available as part of Financial Statements, holdings are calculated / based on the above methodology, namely the post-money valuation of the most recent financing round. This includes, / among others, investments in Axonius in the amount of $15.2 million, Notal Vision in the amount of $15.7 million. / 3 The balance in this item is net of tax. / 4 The balance does not include the investment in Brainsgate.
5 The balance in this item represents the contingent consideration resulting from the sale of CartiHeal to Smith & / Nephew, in accordance with the book value as of December 31, 2025, based on a valuation conducted with the / assistance of an external appraiser.. / 6 The balance in this item primarily represents proceeds expected to be received from the Investments in Creednz Ltd. / and Team8 Surplus (Formerly: Sayata Labs Ltd). For further details see notes 3.B.2.j) and 3.B.2.i) to the Financial / Statements. / 7 The above financial data do not represent a valuation, investment advice, or a financial opinion of any kind.
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Investor Deck //

### Slide 8

**Text**

Meet Elron Management Team: / Knowledge & Experience Combined / to accelerate investment scaling and growth. / Lisya Bahar Manoah / Chairperson / Managing Partner @ Arieli Group. >15 years / of investment and management expertise in / PE & VC, in managing funds, executing M&A / transactions, and leading post-merger / integrations. / Yaniv Shnieder / CEO / Seasoned technology leader with 25 years of / deep tech and defense tech experience. / Founded and led Rafael’s Tel Aviv R&D Center, / building it into a multidisciplinary, startup-style / engineering group of 250 top-tier talents / . / Rony Gur Arie / CFO / Joined Elron Ventures 9 years ago. Oversees the / financial operations and serves as active CFO in / portfolio companies. Broad experience in / accounting, financing, taxation, and securities / regulations. / ELRONVENTURES.COM / Investor Deck // / 8

**Layout**

Three equal columns of headshot, name, role, accent rule and bio sit under a title block; the title block is top-left with a small kicker above it and the logo top-right. Margins are about 4% left and right; a thin full-width rule at 92.5% height separates the content from the footer, with the website left and 'Investor Deck //' and page number 8 right.

**Visuals**

Three grayscale (black-and-white) bust headshots, each about 11.6% of slide width by 28.7% of height, with small rounded corners, sitting in the upper half of each column. Left: Lisya Bahar Manoah, a smiling woman with long dark wavy hair in a blazer over a white top, arms crossed, plain dark-grey blurred backdrop. Centre: Yaniv Shnieder, a smiling man with short hair in a dark button-up shirt with a small embroidered logo on the chest, plain light-grey backdrop. Right: Rony Gur Arie, a smiling woman with shoulder-length brown hair in a white top, soft grey backdrop. All photos are front-facing, head-and-shoulders framing, soft even daylight, not cropped beyond the rounded box. Each photo is boxed with no overlay. Below each name is a blue accent: a short thick bar and a thin rule running to the column edge. Logo: Elron Ventures mark of three stacked rounded horizontal bars in blue with the wordmark Elron / Ventures in blue. No other graphics.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white field: a left text column (x about 4 to 28 percent) holding the title and subtitle, and a right area holding a 5-column by 3-row grid of portrait photos, each with a name and blue role line beneath. The small header 'Meet Elron Team:' sits top-left at about y 5 percent, the Elron Ventures logo top-right, a thin full-width rule near y 93 percent, and a footer with the URL bottom-left and 'Investor Deck // 9' bottom-right; margins about 4 percent on left and right.

**Visuals**

Fifteen black-and-white (greyscale) head-and-shoulders portrait photographs arranged in three rows of five, each boxed with square corners and no border, on a white background. ### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Review: Several key slides are hard to read: the NAV table on slide 7 is tiny with dense footnotes, slide 5 has uneven columns with a very long fifth column, slide 6 states a 20% cut that the table does not show clearly, and slides 10, 21, 28, 34 and 40 are near-empty dividers that add length to a 50-page deck.

**Text**

RDC – Rafael / Development Corporation / ELRONVENTURES.COM

**Layout**

Single-column title slide on a plain white field. The title block is left-aligned at about 18% from the left edge and sits in the vertical middle of the slide, with two lines of title text. The Elron Ventures logo sits top-left at about 4% margin, a thin full-width grey rule runs near the bottom at about 92% height, and the URL footer sits left-aligned below the rule. No slide number is shown. An orange accent bar sits under the title, left-aligned with the title text.

**Visuals**

Logo: Elron Ventures logo at top left, consisting of a blue stylised mark of three horizontal stacked stripes with rounded right ends (an E-like shape) followed by the wordmark 'Elron' on the first line and 'Ventures' on the second line, in blue #508ECA. Accent: a short solid orange bar (about #FF6A00) beneath the title, left-aligned with the title text, approximately 12% of slide width and about 0.6% of slide height thick. Rule: a thin dark grey horizontal line (about #666666, roughly 1px) spanning nearly the full width at about 92.6% of slide height. No photographs, charts or diagrams.

**Design**

Background plain white #FFFFFF with no gradient or texture. Title text is black #000000 in a geometric sans-serif (close to Figtree or Inter Regular), regular weight, sentence/title case, at about 11.85% of slide height per line, with line spacing of roughly 14% of slide height between baselines. Footer URL is black #000000 in a small sans-serif regular, all capitals, at about 1.7% of slide height. Logo blue #508ECA. Accent bar orange #FF6A00. Rule is a thin hairline in grey #666666. No shadows, rounded corners or gradients.

30 years of Investment Partnership:
RAFAEL × Elron Ventures
RDC: Elron and Rafael’s / Partnership Today
Boosting Technological, Product and Market Superiority

TECHNOLOGY / VALIDATION & / DUE DILIGENCE

SPINOFFS / BASED ON / RAFAEL’S IP

DESIGN PARTNERSHIP / WITH PORTFOLIO / COMPANIES
Helping portfolio companies / develop technologies and / mature products in an / enterprise- and military-grade / environment.
Rafael often converts into their / 1st enterprise customer.
GATEWAY INTO / HIGH-ENTRY BARRIER / MARKETS
Rafael can open up markets to / portfolio companies both as a / direct customer, or as a prime / contractor.
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Elron and Rafael’s Partnership

**Layout**

Four equal-width grey cards in a row across the lower half, beneath a full-width orange banner and under a left-aligned title block; a thin rule and three-part footer sit at the bottom. **Visuals**

Four rounded-corner light-grey cards each with a bold uppercase heading, a small blue line icon at top right of the card (1: blue monitor with bar chart and magnifier; 2: blue Rafael winged logo in an oval; 3: blue gear with circuit/nodes; 4: blue hand holding a person-in-circle icon), a short blue horizontal rule with a thicker blue segment at left, and body text. Orange banner has white double chevron arrows at left and right ends. Logos at top right: blue RAFAEL wordmark with blue winged oval emblem, a blue × sign, and blue Elron Ventures logo with stacked stripes. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 12

**Text**

Dual-Use Deep Tech

**Layout**

Three equal columns sit below a left-aligned title that runs across the top-left at about 4% from the left edge; the Elron Ventures logo sits top right. Each column has a company logo at its top, a thin vertical grey rule to its left, a bold name line, then a light description and smaller supporting bullets, all left-aligned; a row of three equal-width category bars sits near the bottom at 81% to 87% of the height, followed by a thin full-width footer rule with 'ELRONVENTURES.COM' bottom left and 'Investor Deck // 12' bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three company logos and one corporate logo, no photographs or charts. Top right: Elron Ventures logo, a stack of three blue horizontal rounded stripes to the left of the blue words 'Elron' and 'Ventures'. Column 1: CyberRidge logo, a blue stepped diagonal mark made of rising blue bars and small diamond squares in lighter blue to pale aqua, above the dark grey wordmark 'CyberRidge'. Column 2: Wonder Robotics logo, a black outlined V-shaped chevron mark at left followed by the wide-spaced black letters 'WONDER' over smaller wide-spaced 'ROBOTICS LTD'. Column 3: Addionics logo, a heavy black italic slanted letter A followed by the bold black capitals 'DDIONICS'. Below the columns: three rectangular category bars with square corners on the left and a rounded top-right corner, two dark grey (#585857) bars flanking one orange (#FA6400) centre bar. Footer: a thin light grey horizontal rule spanning nearly the full width.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with no decoration beyond thin rules and three bars. Titles and body text are black #000000; the company names are bold Arial-like black; accents are orange #FA6400 for the centre bar and dark grey #585857 for the flanking bars and column rules. Typefaces: the title and body are a geometric grotesque sans-serif similar to Figtree, regular weight (title semibold); the column names are Arial Bold. Title is about 5.9% of slide height; column descriptions about 3.0%; supporting bullets about 2.5%; bar labels about 3.9%; footer about 1.7%. Column rules are thin dark grey lines about 0.1% wide. Bars are flat with no shadow and a small corner radius on the top-right. No gradients or shadows.

Four equal portrait columns of headshots sit in the lower-middle band of a 16:9 slide, starting at roughly 2.6%, 31.6%, 56.5% and 81.1% of slide width, with a left title block at the top left and a logo at the top right. The kicker, title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 4.1%, the page title sits below the kicker, and a thin horizontal rule with a footer row (website at left, 'Investor Deck // 13' at right) runs across the bottom at about 92.5% of slide height. The area between about 75% and 92% of slide height is empty.

**Visuals**

Four black-and-white studio headshots, each a rectangular boxed (not cropped to a shape, no border, no shadow) head-and-shoulders portrait. 1) Dr. Liat Shechter Nakash: woman with long dark wavy hair, dark blouse with a white dot pattern, faint closed-mouth smile, looking straight at camera; blurred grey background; framing from mid-chest up, centred; soft even lighting; greyscale. 2) Dr. Moshe Shuker: man with short greying hair, open-collar button shirt, slight smile, looking at camera; plain very light grey/white background; framing shoulders up, centred; soft frontal lighting; greyscale. 3) Moshe Levi: man with short dark hair, dark open-neck shirt, closed-mouth smile, looking at camera; flat mid-grey background; framing shoulders up, centred; soft frontal lighting; greyscale. 4) Imri Landman: man with short greying hair, dark blazer over white open-collar shirt, slight smile, looking at camera; light grey background; framing shoulders up, centred; soft frontal lighting; greyscale. Also: four short thick blue bars (about #4A8BD0) with thin blue hairlines running right from each under the name block, one per column; a thin dark grey (#595959) horizontal rule across the bottom; and a blue Elron Ventures logo at top right consisting of three stacked horizontal slanted stripes (blue #4F8FD0) to the left of the two-line wordmark 'Elron / Ventures' in the same blue.

Role lines are regular sans in blue #508ECA at about 2.2%. Footer text is regular black sans at about 1.7%. Accent: blue #4F8FD0 for logo, role lines and bars. **Text**

DEEP TECH | DEFENSE TECH | CYBERSECURITY | AI
Elron Ventures
A $250B / Opportunity
We create a gateway into high / entry barrier B2G markets
Defense innovation is now seen / as critical to national survival, / strategic competitiveness, and / economic resilience
U.S. FY 2026 NATIONAL / DEFENSE BUDGET / $900.1B
U.S. ALLOCATION TO / PROCUREMENT OF WEAPON / SYSTEMS IN USA / $162B
U.S. ALLOCATION / TO RDT&E IN USA / $146B
EUROPEAN DEFENSE FUND / (EDF) SIZE / €8B
EUROPEAN DEFENCE / INDUSTRY PROGRAMME (EDIP) / €1.5B (Multi-year)
EUROPEAN DEFENSE / EXPENDITURE IN 2024 / €326B
PROJECTED EUROPEAN DEFENSE / EXPENDITURE IN 2025 / €392B
THE NATO SUMMIT (JUNE 2025) APPROVED A / SIGNIFICANT INCREASE IN DEFENSE SPEND / 5% of GDP
VC INVESTMENTS IN DEFENSE TECH ARE / GROWING RAPIDLY / $49.9B in 2025 vs. $37.3B in 2024
ELRONVENTURES.COM

The header line sits top left at y 5%, the Elron Ventures logo top right, and the footer runs along a thin rule at y 92.5% with the website left, sources centred-left, and 'Investor Deck // 20' right. Left margin about 4%, right margin about 4%.

Slide has no photographs or charts. Visual elements: nine rectangular stat boxes in a grid. Row 1 (y 13.5 to 33.4%): three orange boxes #FC6620 with sharp-to-slightly rounded corners, at x 34.4, 55.2 and 76.1%, each 19.8% wide, each holding a small label, a thin white horizontal rule and a large white value. Row 2 (y 35.3 to 55.2%): three dark grey boxes #595959 at the same x positions, with the same label/rule/value structure. Logo: Elron Ventures blue mark (three stacked horizontal rounded bars in #4A8BC9 forming a stylised E) with blue wordmark 'Elron / Ventures' at top right. No photographs, icons or gradients.

### Slide 22

**Text**

Our Micro-Fund: / CYBERFUTURE / Global CISO Investment Alliance / Elron / Ventures / CyberFuture: A Global CISO-Cybersecurity / Investment Vehicle Established by Elron / CyberFuture showcases a select group of top CISOs with / resumes from leading global organizations. / CISO INVESTORS / NETFLIX / Roche / CYBERARK / HiBob / Carlsberg / DOLBY / teva / babylon / PAGAYA / bank hapoalim / leumi / Fireblocks / Amplitude / solaredge / INCD Israel National Cyber Directorate / Vanta / Telefónica / ‘Separating the wheat from the chaff’ in a fragmented space / We are able to identify compelling domains and emerging trends in / collaboration with Rafael’s IT division, CyberFuture, and our security / leader network, who are our ‘ears to the ground’. / GLOBAL TOP-TIER / DEAL FLOW ACCESS / Opportunities to take a / follow-on position in A & B / rounds / BOOSTING MARKET / SUCCESS & EXITABILITY / Product design & positioning, / go-to-market , product- / market fit , brand boost, / access to customers / PORTFOLIO / aryon / Astrix / Prompt / SOLD / CYVERS / entitle / SOLD / CONCENTRIC / zeroport / NEW / RAVEN / NEW / ELRONVENTURES.COM / Elron is the only partner in the partnership that is obligated to finance its expenses and investments, in a total amount of up to $2M. / Investor Deck // 22

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white 16:9 slide with a title block across the top and a full-width horizontal rule dividing the upper content from a portfolio strip. The left column holds the CISO investor logo grid in a light grey panel, and the right column holds a quote heading and two stacked text blocks with orange rules, aligned to a left margin at about 4% and a right margin at about 96%. The 'Our Micro-Fund:' kicker sits top-left, logos sit top-right, a footer rule sits near the bottom with the website left, a disclaimer centred-left, and 'Investor Deck // 22' right-aligned with the slide number.

**Visuals**

Logo grid of 18 company logos in the CISO investors panel, arranged in 4 rows of 4 to 5 items: row 1 NETFLIX, Roche (hexagon outline), CYBERARK (cube icon), HiBob, Carlsberg; row 2 DOLBY, teva, babylon (heart icon), PAGAYA; row 3 bank hapoalim (diamond icon), leumi, Fireblocks (triangle icon), Amplitude; row 4 solaredge, INCD Israel National Cyber Directorate (emblem plus icon), Vanta, Telefónica. All logos are black monochrome on the grey panel. Portfolio strip of 8 logos in a single row: aryon (black circular pattern icon), Astrix (asterisk icon), Prompt (italic, with orange SOLD tag beneath), CYVERS (wifi-style icon), entitle (orange SOLD tag beneath), CONCENTRIC (dotted circle icon), zeroport (Z icon, blue NEW tag beneath), RAVEN (bird icon, blue NEW tag beneath). Top right logos: CYBERFUTURE wordmark with 'Global CISO Investment Alliance' tagline in grey and black, and the Elron Ventures logo in blue (three horizontal stripes with 'Elron Ventures' text). No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with black titles, dark grey #595959 body and subtitle text, orange #FC6620 section labels and accent rules, and blue #4A8BD0 for the Elron logo and NEW tags. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif such as Figtree, medium weight for the title and regular for subtitle and body; the title is about 7.4% of slide height per line, subtitle about 3.0%, body about 2.2%, footer about 1.5%. Logo panel is a flat #F2F2F2 rectangle with slightly rounded corners. Orange accent rules under the quote headings have a thick orange segment fading to a thin line. SOLD tags are orange #F26A21 rotated slightly, NEW tags are blue #4D8BD1 rotated slightly. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white field: a narrow left column (about 0 to 29 percent of width) holds the cybersecurity icon, section label, title, orange subtitle and grey 'Deep Dive' line, stacked top-down from about 36 to 82 percent of height. The right zone (about 29 to 100 percent width, 12.6 to 91.5 percent height) is a single large dark product screenshot that bleeds off the right edge. Logos sit in the top band (RAVEN top-left; CYBERFUTURE and Elron Ventures top-right). A thin grey rule runs across the bottom at about 92.5 percent height with the left footer text ELRONVENTURES.COM and right-aligned 'Investor Deck // 23' beneath it, both at about 94 percent height.

**Visuals**

Product screenshot (photograph of a software screen capture, not a photo of a scene), occupying about 29.4 to 100 percent width and 12.6 to 91.5 percent height, cropped at the right edge, boxed with a hard rectangular edge at top and bottom and a black frame on the bottom edge. It shows a dark-mode web dashboard for an AI agent security product. Left narrow navigation rail with grey outline icons and a highlighted yellow-green icon button. ### Slide 24

**Review notes**

**Text**

RAVEN – New Cybersecurity Investment / Seed and post-seed round of $20M / VISION / To help companies protect their cloud native applications, by being the first solution to achieve visibility into application behavior at runtime - without instrumentation, and without a significant performance penalty. / PRODUCT / Runtime application protection that stops malicious code execution BEFORE it runs. / INVESTMENT PARTNERS / Norwest, Redseed, SentinelOne Ventures, Jibe / OUR INVESTMENT / Led post seed round of - $3.7M through Elron, RDC and CyberFuture / ADDITIONAL INFO / Elron has the right to appoint a board member to the Raven board of directors / ELRONVENTURES.COM / Investor Deck // 24 / Elron Ventures

**Layout**

Four grey rounded-corner panels in a two-row arrangement: a large Vision panel at left and a Product panel at right on the top row, and three equal-height panels (Investment Partners, Our Investment, Additional Info) across the bottom row. **Text**

The global runtime application self-protection (RASP)
market is estimated at $1.9B in 2025 and projected to
grow to $47.55B by 2037 at a 32.8% CAGR
Elron Ventures
Traditional defenses live
outside the application
and can’t understand its
internal logic flow.
Attackers now operate
inside code, libraries,
and execution paths.
Gartner research
MARKET
DEMAND
Cybersecurity teams
are seeking more
effective approaches
to protect
applications during
runtime.
CRITICAL
NEED
The ability to provide a
runtime view and
identify where real risk
lies within an application
will be a primary feature
of application security
solutions.
Sources: https://www.researchnester.com/reports/runtime-application-self-protection-rasp-market/4122; Gartner, Predicts 2026: 4 Forces Reshaping
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Investor Deck // 25
Application and Data Security, 8 January 2026

**Layout**

Three-column layout on a 12-column-style grid with a full-width headline across the top; the title sits at top left (x 2.1%, y 4.5%) with the Elron Ventures logo at top right. The left column holds an orange quote and a Gartner attribution, and two grey rounded cards sit in the centre and right columns with top edges aligned at y 30.8%. Left margin is about 2.1% and right margin about 4.1%. A thin full-width rule sits above the footer at about y 92.5%, with the footer URL at left, the source line in the centre and 'Investor Deck // 25' at right; the slide number 25 is part of the footer text at the right.

**Visuals**

Two grey rounded rectangular cards (fill #F2F2F2) with no border or shadow, each sized about 25.9% by 50.2% of the slide, one at x 35.8% and one at x 64.8%, both at y 30.8%. Each card carries a short blue accent rule under its heading: a thick solid segment about 7% wide followed by a thin hairline running to the card's right edge, in blue #4A86C8 with the thick segment about 3 pt equivalent and the hairline about 0.5 pt. Right of the Gartner research label is a blue (#4A86C8) outlined double chevron icon (two open right-pointing angle brackets, thin stroke) at about x 23.8%, y 76.0%, w 3.0%, h 4.9%. The Elron Ventures logo at top right is a stylised blue (#4A86C8) mark of three stacked horizontal speed-bar strokes with the wordmark 'Elron / Ventures' beside it. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide. Plain white background with black headline, orange quote text, and mid-grey body copy. Title color #000000; quote and attribution in orange #FC6620 (attribution #FA6400); card headings and body in grey #595959; accents and logo in blue #4A86C8; footer text #000000; footer rule grey #404040. Typefaces appear to be a geometric sans-serif similar to Figtree or Outfit: title in semibold/bold, card headings in semibold all caps, quote and body in regular weight, footer in regular. Title text is about 5.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.2%); quote and headings about 4.4%; body about 3.5%; footer about 1.7%. Card shapes have rounded outer corners of about 2% of slide width, no gradients or shadows.

### Slide 26

**Text**

Raven Lives Inside the Application
Raven understands real runtime execution and protects applications from within
Malicious code executes before the world even knows the vulnerability exists in 70% of exploits.
Raven blocks malicious execution at the moment it would run – / whether a CVE (Common Vulnerability and Exposure) exists or not.
Runtime Prevention
Stop malicious code execution / before it ever runs. CVE or no / CVE.
Runtime AI Agents
See every agent. Eliminate rogue / and shadow AI agents.
Runtime Gatekeeper
Predict and block risky / applications before they ever / reach production.
Runtime ADR
Detect and respond to application attacks with deep / application-level forensics.
Runtime SCA
De-prioritize 99% of vulnerabilities, and get / actionable remediation plans for the rest.
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Investor Deck // 26

**Layout**

Single-column header block with the title at top left and the subtitle beneath it, a logo at top right, and a two-row grid of five orange cards in the lower-middle area: three cards in the top row and two wider cards in the bottom row, all inset from the slide edges. The body statement is left-aligned in an indented column starting about 17.5% from the left, and a thin horizontal rule with the footer sits near the bottom, with the footer URL at left and 'Investor Deck // 26' at right.

**Visuals**

Five flat orange rectangles (#FC6620) with small black line icons at their top-left: (1) Runtime Prevention card at x 18.8, y 43.1, 20.8 by 21.6 with a black eagle-head line icon at about x 20.4, y 45.2; (2) Runtime AI Agents card at x 41.1, y 43.1 with a black connected-path icon of a small square and a circle joined by a curved line at about x 43.0, y 45.6; (3) Runtime Gatekeeper card at x 63.4, y 43.1 with a black shield containing a lightning bolt at about x 65.5, y 45.4; (4) Runtime ADR card at x 18.7, y 66.8, 33.3 by 21.6 with a black circular radar/target icon at about x 20.4, y 67.8; (5) Runtime SCA card at x 53.3, y 66.8, 30.8 by 21.6 with a black warning triangle and a black filled circle containing a white check mark at about x 55.5, y 68.2. ### Slide 27

**Text**

STATUS
Co-Investing with CyberFuture in A Leading Category
Raven raises a $20M seed and post-seed round, with a post-seed
investment led by Elron Ventures alongside CyberFuture
The funds will be used to accelerate product development, expand the company’s go-
to-market activities in the United States, and grow the company’s workforce.
Raven has a double-digit number of customers, most of them large enterprises in the
financial services, banking, and insurance sectors, and also operates in additional sectors
that are highly sensitive to application and supply chain incidents.
ELRONVENTURES.COM

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a left text column (title, subtitle and legend) occupies about the left third, and a right-hand horizontal bar chart with a company logo column fills the rest. Title sits top-left at about 8% from the top with a left margin of about 3%. Bars are left-aligned to a vertical axis at about x 34%; the stage band runs along the bottom, and a thin footer rule with URL at bottom-left and Investor Deck // 29 at bottom-right sits at about 92% of height.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart (no numeric axis values; a stage-band replaces an x-axis). Vertical axis: thin grey arrow line at x about 34% running from y about 4% to 83%. Horizontal axis: thin grey arrow line at y about 82.5% from x 34% to about 99%, arrowhead at right end. Categories from top to bottom, each with a logo and a row icon: 1 Axonius, 2 Bitsight, 3 Notal Vision, 4 edge226, 5 OpenLegacy, 6 Addionics, 7 NiTiNOTES, 8 zengo, 9 tamnoon, 10 redaccess, 11 CyberRidge, 12 Cyvers, 13 NDrip, 14 Raven, 15 Bark, 16 Wonder Robotics, 17 Breeze. Bar lengths, estimated against the stage band: Axonius and Bitsight end at about 95% of width (about Series D or beyond, light blue gradient #4A9FCB to #3D8FC4); Notal Vision, edge226 and OpenLegacy end at about 89% (dark navy #0F2F5C to #1F5FA8 gradient); Addionics and NiTiNOTES end at about 75% (navy #1E3F6E to #4A8BC9 gradient); zengo, tamnoon and redaccess end at about 62% (dark grey #3A3A3A to light grey #A0A0A0 gradient); CyberRidge ends at about 62% (same grey gradient); Cyvers ends at about 54% (dark charcoal #333333 solid-looking); NDrip ends at about 54%; Raven, Bark, Wonder Robotics and Breeze end at about 48% (charcoal #2E2E2E). Bars are rounded-end horizontal strips about 1.7% of slide height thick. Stage band (no legend numbers): one segmented gradient bar from x 34% to 97.6%, y 84.6% to 90%, with rounded ends: Seed (black #1A1A1A fading to grey, x 34% to 48%), Round A (grey #4D4D4D to #A6A6A6, x 46.5% to 62%), Round B (blue #1F5FA8 to #5B9BD5, x 60.5% to 75%), Round C (navy #0F2F5C to #1F5FA8, x 75% to 88%), D arrow (light blue #3F8FC9 to #55A8D8, x 87.5% to 97.6%). Logos: Axonius (black wordmark with X-shaped mark), Bitsight (heavy black wordmark), Notal Vision (green-outlined cloud with blue text), edge226 (purple triangle and black text), OpenLegacy (orange and blue square mark with dark text), ADDIONICS (black italic wordmark), NiTiNOTES (blue and green wordmark), zengo (orange Z mark and orange text), tamnoon (green dotted arc mark with dark text), redaccess (red circle mark with black text), CyberRidge (blue stripes above dark wordmark), CYVERS (purple logo above dark caps text), NDrip (green and blue bars with dark text), RAVEN (black bird mark with black text), Bark (navy wordmark with pink mark), WONDER ROBOTICS LTD (black wordmark with small caption), BREEZE (blue-violet italic caps). Row icons: blue rounded squares with a cybersecurity circuit icon (Cybersecurity), light-blue squares with a monitor icon (Medtech), grey squares with a cloud icon (Software), black squares with a shield-and-bug icon (Deep Tech / Defense Tech), dark grey square with star outline (Other). Unicorn icons (black unicorn silhouettes) sit to the left of Axonius and Bitsight. Legend: five rows of icon plus label: Cybersecurity (blue icon, label blue #1E5CA7), Medtech (light blue icon, label #518DCA), Software (grey icon, label #9A9A9A), Deep Tech / Defense Tech (black icon, label #242625), Other (light grey star icon, label #585857). A thin black rule sits under the Legend heading. New badge: orange #F26B0F rounded speech-bubble callout with a small pointer at bottom, white text New, positioned left of the Raven row. Footer rule: thin grey line at about 92.4% height from x 4% to 96%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate investor deck. Background white #FFFFFF. Title Elron Portfolio Snapshot in black #000000, geometric bold sans-serif (Poppins-like), large, about 5.9% of slide height per line, tight leading. Subtitle in black #000000, regular-weight geometric sans, about 3.0% of slide height, line spacing about 1.1. Legend heading black bold Calibri at about 1.9%. Legend labels in Calibri at about 1.85%, colour-coded to their category. Bar chart colours: light blue, navy and greys as described. Stage band labels white Calibri about 2.6%. Footer text black sans-serif about 1.7%. Axis lines mid grey #BCBCBA, about 0.3% thick, with small triangular arrowheads. Bars have rounded ends, no shadows. Orange accent #F26B0F on the New badge. No corner rounding on text. No shadows.

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Portfolio grid organized by colour-coded category with ownership percentage and investment amount on each card.
- What would change the score: Cut or merge the divider slides and several deep-dive pages, and fix the small text on slides 30, 31, 32 and 29 so that investors can read the portfolio at a glance.

**Layout**

Four-column card grid in two rows: Growth Companies (four cards, top) and Early Growth Companies (four cards, below), with the title top-left and the Elron Ventures logo top-right. A legend runs along the bottom right above a grey footer rule with the website at bottom left, footnotes beside it, and 'Investor Deck // 30' at bottom right (page number 30).

**Visuals**

Eight company cards, each with a coloured tab at the left of its header, a company name in blue caps, a company logo at the right of the header, and a description and investment figure in the body. Card 1 BITSIGHT: black unicorn silhouette icon above the header (top-left), BITSIGHT wordmark logo, two asterisks '**' in the body. Card 2 AXONIUS: black unicorn silhouette icon above the header, AXONIUS logo (X-shaped mark over wordmark), '*,' and '**' markers in the body. Card 3 NOTAL VISION: Notal Vision logo (cloud with arrow over wordmark), figures 7% and Our investment $15.0m. Card 4 EDGE 226: edge226 logo (purple-to-magenta triangle mark with wordmark), figures 24% and Our investment $2.5m. Card 5 CYBERRIDGE: CyberRidge logo in a thin black-bordered white box, figures 11%* and Our investment $3.5m +$1m in warrants. Card 6 RED ACCESS: red and black circular logo mark, figures 21%* and Our investment $5.7m. Card 7 TAMNOON: green circle-arc mark over tamnoon wordmark, figures 7%* and Our investment $3.3m. Card 8 BREEZE: blue stylised 'B' mark on a pale blue-white square, figures 13%* and Our investment $2.2m. Legend: five rounded tab swatches with labels, Cybersecurity dark blue, Medtech mid blue, SaaS dark grey, Deep Tech / Defense tech light grey, Other charcoal.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title 'Portfolio Overview' in black #000000 geometric sans-serif (Inter-like), regular weight, about 7.4% of slide height. Section headings in black #000000 sans-serif, medium weight, about 4% of slide height. Company names bold sans-serif in blue #1E5CA7, about 2.6% of slide height. Body text regular sans-serif black #000000, about 2.0% of slide height. Investment figures large regular black about 4.1% of slide height, with 'Our investment' small and amounts bold about 2.4%. Card headers filled #F1F1F1 with thin light grey #D9D9D9 outlines and rounded corners about 2% of slide width on the bottom-right corner, soft shadow none. Colour tabs: Cybersecurity #16457D, Medtech #518DCA, SaaS #585857, Deep Tech #C8C8C8, Other #333735. Footer rule light grey #BFBFBF. Footer text grey #7F7F7F for footnotes, black for website and page label.

**Layout**

Nine company cards sit in a two-row grid, four cards in the top row and four in the bottom row, with the Raven card raised slightly above its row and the Bark card lower, all with left margin about 4% and right margin about 4%. The title Portfolio Overview: sits top-left at about 4.5% from the top, with the subtitle Early Growth Companies below it, the Elron Ventures logo top-right, a legend bottom-right above a thin footer rule near 92% height, and the footer text with ELRONVENTURES.COM left and Investor Deck // 31 right, with three grey footnotes beneath the left footer block.

**Visuals**

Nine company logos, each placed in the top-right of its card header: Cyvers logo, a multicolour stylised wave/arrow mark in purple, blue and green gradient; Wonder Robotics logo, a black angular V-shaped mark with horizontal bars; Zengo logo, an orange interlocking double-loop mark; Raven logo, a black stylised raven head above the word RAVEN in black capitals; Addionics logo, black slanted letter A followed by the word ADDIONICS in bold black capitals; OpenLegacy logo, an orange square-pixel mark above the word OpenLegacy in dark navy; Nitinotes logo, the word NiTiNOTES in white capitals on a solid blue #3D7BF5 rectangle; Bark logo, the word Bark in dark navy with a small pink-magenta dash mark; the Elron Ventures logo at top-right, a blue stacked-stripe mark in #4A90D9 beside the blue text Elron Ventures. Each card has a dark-coloured half-round tab at its left edge in the category colour (Cybersecurity navy, Medtech blue, Deep Tech light grey, SaaS dark grey, Other charcoal). Legend row at bottom right: five rounded tab swatches in navy #16457D, blue #518DCA, grey #585857, light grey #C0C0C0 and charcoal #333735, each followed by its label. A small blue speech-bubble tag labelled New sits above the top-right corner of the Raven card. No charts or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Titles and card names use a dark black #000000 for the main title and a strong blue #1E5CA7 for company names. Body text is black #000000 and the investment figures are bold black. Accents are the category colours: navy #16457D, medtech blue #518DCA, grey #585857, and charcoal #333735. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Inter or SF Pro, regular weight for body text and bold for company names and amounts; the main title is bold and roughly 7.4% of slide height, subtitle about 4% of slide height, card body text about 2% of slide height. Cards are white rectangles with light grey #D0D0D0 hairline outlines and rounded corners on the right side; card headers are filled #F1F1F1 with the left category tab. Footnotes are small grey #7F7F7F text. A thin light grey rule runs across the bottom at about 92.3% height.

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Cut or merge the divider slides and several deep-dive pages, and fix the small text on slides 30, 31, 32 and 29 so that investors can read the portfolio at a glance.

**Text**

Portfolio Company News
Elron Ventures
CyberRidge reports business progress
and industry recognition
CyberRidge completed a successful POC at Vodafone’s
optical network lab and announced a strategic
collaboration with FuRi Connect. The company was also
referenced in Gartner research on “Harvest Now,
Decrypt Later” attacks and named a finalist in the 2026
SC Awards for Best Emerging Technology
Breeze Security named among
innovators in Gartner research on
Preemptive Exposure Management
Breeze Security recognized by Gartner as an
innovator in Preemptive Exposure Management
OpenLegacy partners with AWS
OpenLegacy announced the launch of OpenLegacy
Hub, a modernization platform built on and
integrated with AWS to help enterprises modernize
mainframe and legacy applications incrementally
without downtime
Notal Vision receives CMS
reimbursement pricing
CMS establishes reimbursement pricing for Notal
Vision’s SCANLY Home OCT remote retinal monitoring
service for AMD patients, effective 2026
Axonius receives two industry
recognitions
Axonius named one of Forbes’ America’s Best
Startup Employers 2026 and received a 5-Star
rating in CRN’s 2026 Partner Program Guide,
alongside inclusion in the CRN Security 100
Wonder Robotics launches IroNav with
infiniDome
Wonder Robotics partners with infiniDome to
introduce IroNav, a resilient autonomy and navigation
solution for drones and VTOL platforms
Astrix Security featured in Gartner
Market Guide
Astrix Security recognized as a Representative
Vendor in Gartner’s inaugural Market Guide for
Guardian Agents
CyberFuture invests in Zeroport
CyberFuture, Elron Ventures’ cybersecurity micro
fund, invested in Zeroport’s $10M seed round
supporting its non-IP remote access technology
Aryon Security named among Top 10
Cybersecurity Startups to Watch
Aryon Security recognized as one of the Top 10 most
watched cybersecurity startups of 2025 by Startup
Nation Central.
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Investor Deck // 32

**Layout**

Three-column grid of nine news items in three rows, with the title “Portfolio Company News” top-left at about 3% left margin and the Elron Ventures logo top-right. Each item stacks logo, bold headline, then grey body text, left-aligned to its column; a thin grey rule spans the width near the bottom, with the website address bottom-left and “Investor Deck // 32” bottom-right as footer, page number 32 included.

**Visuals**

Nine company logos, each placed above its item: CyberRidge wordmark (dark grey text with blue-grey stacked-bar mark above it); BREEZE wordmark (white italic capitals on a royal-blue #0A5CFF rectangle with darker blue gradient edge); OpenLegacy logo (orange #F26B21 bracket-square mark with blue #3B3FA0 wordmark); Notal Vision logo (green #6CB33F outline cloud with up arrow above light-blue #5BC0DE wordmark); Axonius logo (black cross-like node mark with one orange dot above widely spaced black capitals AXONIUS); Wonder Robotics logo (black stylised W mark with spaced black WONDER and smaller ROBOTICS LTD); Astrix logo (blue asterisk mark with blue #1E6FE8 wordmark Astrix); Zeroport logo (purple #7B3FE4 Z mark with black zeroport wordmark); Aryon logo (black circular striped mark with black aryon wordmark). Plus the Elron Ventures stacked logo at top right (blue #4A90D9 horizontal stripe icon with Elron / Ventures wordmark). A thin grey horizontal divider line runs across the lower part of the slide. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 34

**Review notes**

- Review: Several key slides are hard to read: the NAV table on slide 7 is tiny with dense footnotes, slide 5 has uneven columns with a very long fifth column, slide 6 states a 20% cut that the table does not show clearly, and slides 10, 21, 28, 34 and 40 are near-empty dividers that add length to a 50-page deck.

**Text**

Select Portfolio
Companies
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Elron
Ventures
Investor Deck // 34

**Layout**

Single-title section slide on a white field. The title sits left-aligned at about 18% from the left edge, vertically around the middle of the slide, with a short orange rule beneath it. The Elron Ventures logo sits in the top-right corner. A thin full-width horizontal rule sits near the bottom, with the footer website at bottom left and 'Investor Deck // 34' with the slide number at bottom right, both on one baseline.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: (1) Elron Ventures logo, top right: a stylised mark of three stacked horizontal, slightly slanted blue bars forming an 'E'-like shape to the left of the two-line wordmark 'Elron' / 'Ventures' in a blue sans-serif. (2) Short solid orange horizontal bar under the title, about 7% of slide width and under 1% of slide height, left-aligned with the title's left edge. (3) Thin dark grey horizontal rule spanning nearly the full slide width near the bottom, above the footer. No photographs, charts, or diagrams.

**Design**

Background pure white #FFFFFF with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title text is black #000000 in a geometric sans-serif regular weight (closest to Figtree or Inter Regular), sentence/title case, about 11.9% of slide height, with generous line spacing (about 1.0 line pitch of 15% of slide height between the two title lines). Footer text is black #000000 in the same sans-serif, regular weight, about 1.7% of slide height, uppercase for the website. Accent colour is orange #FF6A00 for the title underline; logo is blue #4A90D9. Rule is a hairline about 1 px in dark grey #444444. Shapes are flat with no corner radius, shadow or gradient.

OpenLegacy logo wordmark / OpenLegacy / SOFTWARE / OpenLegacy / Deep Dive / ELRONVENTURES.COM / Investor Deck // 35 / Elron Ventures

**Layout**

Full-bleed white slide with a left-aligned title block (SOFTWARE label, large OpenLegacy title, Deep Dive subtitle) at the left third, and a centred illustration spanning the right three-quarters. The OpenLegacy logo sits top-left and the Elron Ventures logo top-right, both near the top margin. A thin horizontal rule runs across the lower part of the slide above a footer row with the website at left and 'Investor Deck // 35' at right; the slide number 35 is in the footer.

**Visuals**

One illustration, a network diagram: on the left, four identical white rounded-square cards each holding a navy (#3A4BA0) monitor icon with a user silhouette, stacked vertically at x about 27 to 30%. Each card has a column of small navy dots at its right edge, from which many thin pale-grey curved lines fan out and converge into a single point at the left edge of a central circle. The central circle is a pale peach (#FBE6D6) disc with a thin light-grey ring around it broken by gaps, and holds the OpenLegacy orange (#E8741C) and navy/cyan square-bracket mark. On the right, lines fan out from the circle's right side to four identical white rounded-square cards each holding an orange (#E8741C) cloud-server icon, with orange dotted edges. Some lines are highlighted in blue-violet (#4A5BD0) short streaks. Faded, blurred, low-opacity copies of the navy speech-bubble icons and orange cloud icons float at the far left and far right edges as background decoration. Beside the title is a grey (#9E9E9E) rounded pill (left-edge half-circle shape) containing a white outline cloud with a gear. The whole illustration is unboxed and sits on the white background, not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title OpenLegacy in a black (#000000) regular-weight geometric sans-serif (close to Figtree or Inter), roughly 8.15% of slide height, sentence case. Label SOFTWARE in dark charcoal (#242625) regular sans-serif uppercase about 3.7% of slide height. Subtitle Deep Dive in mid grey (#595959) regular sans-serif about 3.45% of slide height. Footer text in black sans-serif about 1.73% of slide height, uppercase for the website. Accents: OpenLegacy orange #E8741C, navy #3A4BA0, cyan #3CC4DE, Elron blue #4A90D9. Footer rule is a thin dark grey line about 1 px. Pill has fully rounded left end, no shadow.

### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Standard deep-dive template with vision, product, investment and partners blocks that is repeated for each featured company.
- Worth keeping: Keep the orange and blue palette, the Vision, Product, Partners, Investment and Additional Info template on slides 24, 36, 41 and 46, and the market-number tiles on slide 20.

**Text**

OPENLEGACY
Elron
Ventures
VISION
To unlock the value of legacy systems by making
them, and the data they hold, fully accessible to
modern digital applications, accelerating
innovation without risking business continuity.
PRODUCT
AI-driven platform for moving critical on-
premise systems (mainframes, AS/400) from
legacy to modern cloud-native environments
through a phased and risk-free approach.
INVESTMENT PARTNERS
SBI Holdings, Silverhorn, Leumi
Partners
OUR INVESTMENT
$8.2M through RDC*
ADDITIONAL INFO
RDC has the right to appoint two
board members to the
OpenLegacy board of directors
ELRONVENTURES.COM
* Does not include $0.8 relating to RDC’s SAFE investment signed in March 2026, which had not yet been transferred to the company. For
further details see notes 3.B.2p) to the Financial Statements.
Investor Deck // 36

**Layout**

Single-slide grid: a large title OPENLEGACY sits top-left at the top margin, with the Elron Ventures logo top-right. Below, a 2-row layout: two large grey cards on top (VISION left, wider and taller; PRODUCT right, slightly offset higher) and three equal-width cards in the bottom row (INVESTMENT PARTNERS, OUR INVESTMENT, ADDITIONAL INFO). Each card has a centred or left-aligned blue heading, an orange-and-thin-line divider, and body text. A thin horizontal footer rule sits near the bottom with ELRONVENTURES.COM left, a footnote centre-left, and 'Investor Deck // 36' right-aligned at the bottom margin. No separate slide-number placeholder beyond the footer text.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: (1) Five light grey rounded rectangle cards, fill #EDEDED, with small corner radius and no shadow. (2) Three thin orange divider rules, one under each card heading, plus one under VISION and PRODUCT: each is a hairline (about 1 px) orange line spanning about 25 to 38 percent of slide width, centred under the heading, with a thicker solid orange bar (about 7 to 11 percent of slide width, about 1 percent of slide height) centred on it. (3) Thin dark grey horizontal footer rule across the full content width near 92.5 percent of slide height. (4) Elron Ventures logo at top right: an icon of three stacked horizontal rounded bars in blue, slanted, followed by the wordmark Elron over Ventures in blue. No photographs, charts, tables or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 37

**Text**

The average global enterprise wastes more than $370M / annually due to being unable to efficiently modernize / outdated legacy systems / Elron / Ventures / Mainframe modernization projects, meanwhile, delivered between 288% and 362% ROI in 2025 / Legacy systems are the backbone of the enterprise, but they face critical structural / hurdles in today's digital economy / 70% / 70-75% / 48% / 76% / of Fortune 500 / companies still operate / software over two / decades old / of IT spending can get / consumed by legacy / technologies / of organizations cite / complexity as their top / modernization / challenge / of IT leaders find it / challenging to access / mainframe data, making it / a goldmine of untapped / potential for AI platforms / Sources: https://www.openlegacy.com/blog/mainframe-application-modernization; https://www.dreamfactory.com/hub/legacy-system-modernization- / statistics; https://www.rocketsoftware.com/sites/default/files/resource_files/transform-ai-and-analytics.pdf / ELRONVENTURES.COM / Investor Deck // 37

**Layout**

Four equal statistic cards in a row centred under a centred subheading, with the bold two-line headline left-aligned at the top-left margin and a subtitle beneath it. A thin rule runs across the footer above a left footer URL, a centred-left sources block, and a right-aligned Investor Deck // 37 page label.

**Visuals**

Four statistic blocks, none of them charts. Each has a large bold blue figure (70%, 70-75%, 48%, 76%) in Arial Bold #2B66AC, centred above a thin orange-red (#F26522-like) horizontal rule with a thicker short orange bar at its centre, and a light grey rectangle below holding the caption text. No photographs, icons or illustrations. Elron Ventures logo at top right: a stylised blue (#4A90D9-like) striped mark of three horizontal bars with a slanted left edge, beside the words Elron / Ventures in blue sans-serif.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Headline and body are dark sans-serif (Figtree-like geometric sans), headline in bold black #000000, about 5.4% of slide height per line. Subtitle in regular #595959 at about 3.5% height. Central statement in medium-weight grey-slate #6B7280 at about 4.0% height. Statistic figures in bold Arial #2B66AC at about 4.9% height. Card captions in regular black #000000 at about 3.0% height, centred. Footer text about 1.7% height in black. Orange accent #F26522 used for rule and bar. Cards are light grey #EDEDED filled rectangles with slightly rounded bottom corners and no visible borders or shadows, each about 21% wide and 22.4% tall. Footer separated by a thin dark horizontal rule.

### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Four-part feature layout with a large statistic and a short label per benefit, which turns a product claim into a clear visual.

**Text**

Solving the Mainframe Modernization Decoupling Challenge
OpenLegacy Hub transforms legacy bottlenecks into integration artifacts with automated, risk-free integration
Elron Ventures
THE COST BARRIER
-200%
COST
Automated Factory
Replaces manual coding with auto-generated connectors, eliminating human error and rework.

THE RISK FACTOR
NO
FAILURE

**Layout**

Three equal white cards sit in a row, centred horizontally between about 14.6% and 80% of the slide width, with the top of each card at about 26% and the bottom at about 73%. Each card has a tinted header band in its upper 40%, a white body below it, and a coloured pill badge hanging below the card. The title is top-left at about 4.4% from the top with a left margin of about 2.1%; the subtitle sits beneath it, left-aligned at about 12.1%; a small logo is top-right at about 88.7% x and 4.4% y. A thin full-width hairline sits at about 92.5% of the height, with the website bottom-left and 'Investor Deck // 38' bottom-right, both on about 94% of the height and inset about 4% from each side.

**Visuals**

Three cards in a row, each with an icon, a large figure and a label, a headline, a body paragraph, a progress bar and a badge. Card 1 (orange): a white circle with an orange hand holding a dollar sign icon at top centre; header text THE COST BARRIER, figure -200% with grey COST label; a small orange downward arrow in a white circle; headline Automated Factory; body text; a white rounded box containing Automation with 90% in orange and an orange progress bar spanning about 17.6% to 32.1% of the slide width at about 72% height; below, an orange pill (tag icon, 60% SAVINGS). Card 2 (sky blue): a white circle with a blue warning triangle; header THE RISK FACTOR, figure NO with grey FAILURE label; a small blue downward arrow in a white circle; headline Standard & Secure; body text; a light blue rounded box containing Risk Reduction with Max in sky blue and a sky-blue progress bar spanning about 41% to 55% of the slide width; below, a sky-blue pill with a white shield icon and ZERO DISRUPTIONS. Card 3 (indigo): a white circle with an indigo hourglass icon; header THE TIME TRAP, figure 10X with grey FASTER label; a small indigo downward arrow in a white circle; headline Acceleration; body text; a light lavender rounded box containing Velocity with 10x in indigo and an indigo progress bar spanning about 64.4% to 78.9% of the slide width; below, an indigo pill with a white lightning bolt icon and FROM MONTHS TO WEEKS. The elements are not cropped. The card background frames are soft drop shadows. The hairline footer rule is dark grey.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no gradient or texture. Main title is black #000000 bold geometric sans (Inter-like) at about 5.4% of slide height, single line. Subtitle is regular-weight sans in grey #595959 at about 3.5%. Card headlines are bold Arial-like sans in indigo #3C4DA7 at about 3.0%. Card header labels are bold uppercase Arial-like in grey #6B7280 at about 2.2%. Large figures are bold Arial-like in near-black #1F2937 at about 3.4%, with small bold uppercase grey #9CA3AF labels at about 2.0%. Body copy is regular sans in grey #6B7280 at about 2.2%, centred. Accent colours: orange #E77207, sky blue #3DB9E6, indigo #3C4DA7. Card headers are tinted #FFF7ED (orange card), #EFF6FF (blue card) and #EEF2FF (indigo card). Cards have a faint drop shadow and slightly rounded corners of about 2% of width. Progress bars are about 0.7% of slide height tall with rounded ends. Badges are fully rounded pills with bold uppercase white text at about 1.7%. Footer text is black regular sans at about 1.7%.

A thin full-width rule near the bottom carries a footer row: site URL at left, disclaimer after it, and 'Investor Deck // 39' right-aligned, which is the slide number.

**Visuals**

Logo: the Elron Ventures mark, a blue icon of three stacked horizontal slanted bars (about 4% wide, 2.5% tall at x 88.8, y 5.3) with the wordmark 'Elron / Ventures' in blue to its right, set in two lines. Column headings: medium-weight uppercase grey #595959 sans, about 2.5% of height. Body text: regular-weight grey #595959 sans, about 2.5% of height, with tight line spacing of roughly 1.2 times the size. Accent colour: orange #F26A21 for the rules; brand blue #4A90D9 for the logo. Footer text: near-black #000000 at about 1.7% of height. Rules are square-ended with no corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Text**

tamnoon / Elron Ventures / CYBERSECURITY / Tami / Your New Cloud SecOps / Agent from tamnoon / Tamnoon / Deep Dive / ELRONVENTURES.COM / Investor Deck // 40

**Layout**

Plain white slide with no visible grid. Tamnoon wordmark logo sits top-left and Elron Ventures logo top-right, both near the top margin. A category label with blue icon tile sits at left-middle, with the large title 'Tamnoon' and subtitle 'Deep Dive' stacked left-aligned below it. A wide Tami banner image sits on the right half at the same height as the category label, running to the right slide edge. A thin full-width grey rule sits near the bottom with footer text ELRONVENTURES.COM at left and 'Investor Deck // 40' with slide number at right.

Icon: square-rounded blue tile (#1E5CA7) at left edge containing a white line icon of a briefcase/device with circuit-like nodes and connectors. Overlaid white bold italic text 'Tami' with a green sparkle, a thin white vertical divider, and white bold sans text 'Your New Cloud SecOps / Agent from' followed by the green-and-white tamnoon logo.

### Slide 42

**Text**

According to Gartner, spending on cloud security is expected / to grow to $31.5B in 2029, at a CAGR of 25% / Elron Ventures / Tamnoon addresses a critical market need / As cloud usage continues to grow, so does the burden of ensuring its security. Cloud teams are overwhelmed by / numerous, vague cloud security alerts, leading to slow, manual remediation and increased security risk. / Alert Fatigue & Noise / Slow Remediation / Lack of Context / Bridging Security & / Engineering / An average-sized / organization can receive / 1,000+ cloud security alerts / each week / It takes an average of 145 / hours (around six days) to / resolve a cloud security alert, / with a shortage of qualified / staff often cited as the main / challenge / Most teams are hesitant to / trust AI with making changes / in production, largely because / critical context is often stored / in the minds of human experts / Security alerts are not being / translated into actionable / engineering tasks, leading to / communication challenges / between cybersecurity / personnel and engineers / ELRONVENTURES.COM / Sources: Gartner, Sector Overview: Information Security and Risk Management, 4Q25, 1 December 2025; https://tamnoon.io / Investor Deck // 42

**Layout**

Full-width title block at top left spanning two lines, with the Elron Ventures logo at top right beside the title. Below the title a blue subtitle and a grey body paragraph run left-aligned at about 2.3% margin, followed by four equal-width grey cards in a row across the lower-middle of the slide, each with a bold heading above it. A thin rule sits above a footer row with the URL at left, source line beside it, and 'Investor Deck // 42' at right.

**Visuals**

Four light grey rounded-bottom rectangular cards (#EDEDED) arranged in a row, each with a bold Arial heading above and a thin orange (#FF6B1A-like, about #F26522) horizontal line with a thicker orange bar segment centred beneath each heading as a decorative accent. Card 1 heading 'Alert Fatigue & Noise' with text about 1,000+ alerts per week; card 2 'Slow Remediation' about 145 hours; card 3 'Lack of Context' about AI trust; card 4 'Bridging Security & Engineering' about alerts not translated into engineering tasks. No charts, photos or icons other than the Elron Ventures logo: a stylised blue stack of three horizontal bars (#508ECA) with the words 'Elron Ventures' beside it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 43

**Text**

"Supervised autopilot" for cloud security:
Elron Ventures
Blending AI efficiency with human intelligence to deliver faster, more effective cloud risk reduction
Tamnoon provides a managed cloud security remediation service that uses AI and human experts (CloudPros) to
prioritize, investigate, and fix security alerts and misconfigurations in the organization’s existing tools, turning noisy
alerts into actionable tasks and preventing future exposures
Prioritize
The platform
aggregates all cloud
security alerts, making
them manageable.
Investigate & Suggest
Tami, the AI Cloud Security
Agent, enriches each alert with
context it captures from
analyzing metadata, performs
initial investigations, identifies
root causes, and suggests safe,
custom-made remediation plans.
Remediate
CloudPros, a team of
human cloud security
experts, supervise Tami's
actions, verify remediation
plans, and handle complex
incidents.
Prevent
Every remediation
performed by
Tamnoon also comes
with a prevention plan.
Results for Users:
90% Reduction in Effective Risk Exposure |
0 Criticals in 90 Days or Less |
1 Hour Per Week Spent on Security Alerts
ELRONVENTURES.COM
This slide contains forward-looking information as defined in Section 32A of the Israel Securities Law, 5728-1968. See slide 2 of this presentation.
Investor Deck // 43

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a white field with the title top-left at about 4% from the left edge, a subtitle beneath it, and a centred-left body paragraph at about 5% left margin. Four equal-width text columns (Prioritize, Investigate & Suggest, Remediate, Prevent) sit in the middle band, each marked by a thin vertical orange rule at its left edge, followed by a bold results headline and a single results line above a thin footer rule; the footer carries a left-aligned URL, a disclaimer, and a right-aligned 'Investor Deck // 43' slide number, with the logo at top right.

**Visuals**

Four thin vertical orange (#F26B2A) rules, one at the left of each column heading, spanning from the heading down to the bottom of each column's text (about 24 percent of the slide height each). One Elron Ventures logo at top right: a blue (#4A90D9) stacked mark of three horizontal wave-like bars to the left of the two-line wordmark 'Elron / Ventures'. A thin dark grey (#404040) horizontal footer rule across the width of the slide near 92.5 percent height. No photographs, charts, or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Title and body text black (#000000); the main title is a bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) at about 5.4 percent of slide height. Subtitle is a regular geometric sans (Figtree-like), grey #595959, about 3.5 percent of height. Column headings are Arial Bold in blue #1E5CA7 at about 3.5 percent of height. Column body text is regular Figtree-like sans in mid grey #898989 at about 2.5 percent of height. Accent numbers 90%, 0 and 1 are Arial Bold in orange #FC6620; the rest of the results line is regular black at about 3.0 percent. Footer text is about 1.7 percent of height, black. Lines are thin, square-ended, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners.

**Layout**

Single-column header with the title STATUS in the top-left at about 2% from the left edge, a subtitle beneath it, and an Elron Ventures logo top-right. The upper half holds two columns: left-aligned body text (about 6% from the left edge) beside a robot illustration and a WIN award certificate; a full-width orange rule at about 61% of height separates it from a row of four bold stat blocks on the left and a pie chart on the right. A thin grey footer rule at about 93% height carries the footer line with the URL at left, the disclaimer in the centre and 'Investor Deck // 44' at right.

**Visuals**

Four non-text visuals. (1) Illustration, upper-centre, about x 48 to 69%: a cartoon blue robot head with a dark visor and two lime-green eye bars, with lime-green and blue flared legs, standing on a grey ground shadow. Around it float about 12 small circular app icons (aws, a cloud, a red bullseye, a blue A, a red-orange glyph, a white hexagon with red outline, a teal ring, a purple hexagon, a red bar, a red spiral, a teal swirl, a green ring, a dark ring), arranged in a loose ring on a pale yellow starburst; white background, no crop, no box. (2) Certificate image, upper-right, boxed with square corners: a saturated royal blue (#0B5BE0 approximately) rectangle with a lighter blue sunburst border and a thin white inner frame; at top-left a small white WIZ logo; centred 'WINvaluable Award' in pink-magenta and white, 'Presented to:' in small white, the word 'tamnoon' in white bold wordmark; a round blue badge with a white WIN seal at top-right and a light-blue ribbon tail below it; a light blue band across the middle holding a blue two-line message; white cloud shapes at the lower left and right corners; 'WITH' and a pink heart above italic 'THE WIN TEAM' at bottom centre. (3) Pie chart image, lower-right, a circle of about 12 equal-looking slices with a thin dark outline, slices clockwise from top-right: royal blue slice with a white WIZ logo; pale pink slice with a magenta logo and a black dot; red slice with a white bird-like logo; blue slice with a dark ring logo; orange-red slice with a white stacked-bars logo; black slice with the four-colour Microsoft logo; light grey slice with a green padlock-shield icon; light grey slice with a red starburst icon; red slice with a grey and red cluster icon; green slice with a dark ring icon; dark navy slice with a blue shield icon; very pale grey slice with a dark U-shaped icon. Logo identities beyond WIZ and Microsoft are approximate. (4) Orange horizontal rule, full width of the content area, about 0.3% of height thick, colour #FC6620. No charts with numeric values are present.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no texture. Title STATUS in black (#000000) regular-to-medium geometric sans-serif (Hanken Grotesk-like), all caps, large at about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle 2025 ACHIEVEMENTS in orange #FC6620, regular-weight sans-serif in all caps at about 4.4% of height. Body text in black #000000, regular sans-serif at about 3.0% of height, with bold labels in Arial Bold, black. Stat blocks in Arial Bold #2B66AC, about 3.5% of height, left-aligned. Accent colour orange #FC6620 for subtitle and divider; blue #2B66AC for stats; Elron logo in steel blue #5B8FCB. Footer text in black at about 1.7% of height. Line styles: orange divider solid about 1px; footer rule thin dark grey (#555555) solid. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on text or rules.
