**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left text column holds the logo, icon, section label, title and subtitle, and a dark illustration panel fills the right side from about 45% of the width to the right edge and from about 11% to 89% of the height. The CyberRidge logo sits top-left and the Elron Ventures logo top-right, both at the top margin. A thin full-width horizontal rule sits near the bottom at about 92.5% height, with the footer line below it: ELRONVENTURES.COM flush left and 'Investor Deck // 45' flush right, both at about 94% height. The slide number 45 is inside the right footer text.

**Visuals**

Photograph-free technical line illustration: an isometric wireframe drawing of a rack-mounted optical hardware unit, rendered as thin light grey/white (#E6E6E6) outlines on a near-black (#141414) panel. Components: a flat rectangular top cover slab at upper centre, outlined with a thin edge; a cylindrical stack of rings with green, yellow and grey bands (labelled Optical encryptor) at upper centre; a rectangular box with a vertical face of yellow, orange and red stripes (labelled Noise Loading) at left; a flat plate with a fan of red and magenta lines (labelled Pulse Laser) at right; a smaller block with a fan of blue and violet lines (labelled Coherent Transceiver) at lower centre; a front panel at lower right with rows of small square ports and buttons, a diagonal set of small diamond keys, and a rounded handle at the right end. Leader lines are thin white strokes ending in small white diamond dots on each component; the labels are white monospace text. The illustration is full-panel, unboxed inside its dark rectangle, not cropped, with flat even lighting and no overlay.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 46

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the orange and blue palette, the Vision, Product, Partners, Investment and Additional Info template on slides 24, 36, 41 and 46, and the market-number tiles on slide 20.

**Text**

CYBERRIDGE
Elron Ventures
VISION
To change the cybersecurity game rules with
physics by removing data from the game, and
protect the world’s most sensitive data from
interception, surveillance, and quantum-era
decryption.
PRODUCT
A quantum-safe solution for transmission of
data over public infrastructure, which
prevents data interception by making the
signal unrecordable using photonic encryption
technology.
INVESTMENT PARTNERS
AWZ, Arkin Capital, Redseed VC
OUR INVESTMENT
$3.5M through Elron and RDC
in equal proportions
ADDITIONAL INFO
Elron has the right to appoint an
observer to the CyberRidge board
of directors
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Investor Deck // 46

### Slide 47

**Text**

The quantum security market is projected to grow from
~$0.7B today to ~$10B by 2030, at a CAGR of over 50%
Quantum computers are expected to be powerful enough to weaken and break today’s cryptographic algorithms
There is a consensus that organizations need to prepare for post-quantum cryptography (PQC) without delay, as a response to ‘harvest now, decrypt later’ threats.
‘Harvest now, decrypt later’ = Attackers steal and store encrypted data today, betting that they'll be able to decrypt it once quantum computing matures
Gartner predicts that by 2029, advances in quantum computing will make conventional asymmetric cryptography unsafe to use, and states that launching a post-quantum program is urgent, claiming that the window for proactive action is closing.
The Problem:
PQC too is vulnerable to "Harvest Now, Decrypt Later" attacks, because it still transmits raw, encrypted data across the network.
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Sources: https://thequantuminsider.com/2024/11/27/report-reveals-growing-interest-investments-in-quantum-security-market/; Gartner, Postquantum Cryptography: The Time to Prepare Is Now! 1 July 2024; Gartner, Justify, Build and Launch a Postquantum Response, 12 November 2024
This slide contains forward-looking information as defined in Section 32A of the Israel Securities Law, 5728-1968. See slide 2 of this presentation.
Investor Deck // 47

A horizontal orange rule separates the argument block from a 'The Problem:' statement, and a thin footer rule sits above a three-line source block with the site name at bottom left and 'Investor Deck // 47' at bottom right; there is no separate slide number beyond that footer text.

No photographs, charts, tables or diagrams.

### Slide 48

**Text**

No way to harvest & hack data offline = no exposure
Elron Ventures
CyberRidge prevents Harvest-Now-Decrypt-Later attacks, by making data-in-transit unrecordable and / quantum-resilient from day one
CyberRidge transforms critical data over high-capacity public infrastructure / into optical noise that no computer power can process
The signal is spread / over a huge optical / spectrum
Optical features / are modified by / the optical key
The signal is / buried below / optical noise
• No algorithm, AI model, or quantum machine can decrypt the data because there is / no decipherable signal to begin with – only meaningless optical noise.
• The only way to recompose the data is with a proprietary photonic key that changes / every fraction of a second and must be present at the exact moment the light signal / arrives. Without it, the data is lost forever.
ELRONVENTURES.COM
Investor Deck // 48

**Layout**

Left-aligned title block across the top with a logo at top right; a three-stage horizontal process row (icon, bold blue label, chevron/orange divider, repeated) in the upper-middle; a large orange statement below it; a thin rule above a bulleted body text at lower left and a dark diagram image at lower right; a footer rule with URL at bottom left and 'Investor Deck // 48' at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three small icons in a row with arrows between them: (1) a grey/black vertical-bar spectrum graphic of varying-height bars in dark grey, mid grey and light grey, representing a signal spread over a wide spectrum; (2) a black square panel with a row of coloured bars (blue, purple, magenta) leading into a white key-shaped silhouette with a black dot, representing optical features modified by an optical key; (3) a black cylinder-like pipe with end caps containing a rectangular grid of multicoloured pixels (magenta, red, orange, pink, purple), representing the signal buried in optical noise. Two thin orange vertical divider lines separate the stages and black chevron arrows point right between them. At lower right, a 805x608 technical line-drawing illustration on a near-black background with thin white/grey outline strokes of an isometric device: a flat rectangular base-plate and a box-shaped unit with labelled callouts 'Noise Loading' (left, pointing to a small box with coloured stripes), 'Optical encryptor' (top, pointing to a cylinder with green/red rings), 'Pulse Laser' (right, pointing to a flat rectangle with coloured stripes), and 'Coherent Transceiver' (bottom left, pointing to a grey module); the device front has a row of small buttons and a dark panel. Image is boxed, not cropped, with no overlay. A short black horizontal rule (about 1.3% height, thin) sits above the bullets.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in black (#000000) bold sans-serif, approximately Helvetica/Arial-like bold, title cap height large relative to slide. Subtitle body in grey #595959 regular sans-serif, about 3.5% of slide height. Orange statement in #FC6620 regular sans-serif (Figtree-like geometric sans), about 4.9% of slide height. Stage labels in bold Arial #1E5CA7 blue, about 3% of slide height. Bullet body text black #000000 regular sans-serif about 3% of slide height. Footer text black, about 1.7% of slide height. Thin orange vertical divider lines (#FC6620) and black chevrons and rules. No shadows, no gradients, no corner radii on images.

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a white slide with a large left-aligned title at top left and a logo at top right. A thin horizontal rule sits above the footer, which carries the website at left, a forward-looking statement in the centre-left, and the deck name and slide number at right. Two boxed logo images and one vertical rule, no charts. Corner logo at top right: Elron Ventures mark, three stacked blue horizontal chevron-like bars (#5B9BD5) with the words 'Elron' and 'Ventures' in blue to the right. No photographs or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with black text. Title 'STATUS' in black geometric sans-serif (Figtree-like), medium weight, all capitals, about 7.4 percent of slide height. Body bullets in black geometric sans-serif (Figtree-like) regular weight, about 4.9 percent of slide height, with generous line spacing (about 6 percent of slide height between lines) and wide gaps between bullet groups. Accent colours: orange rule #E8552B, blue logo #5B9BD5, navy Gartner box #1B2F5B. Footer text is black sans-serif about 1.7 percent of slide height. Hairline grey footer rule (#555555). ### Slide 50

**Text**

Elron Ventures / Thank You! / ELRONVENTURES.COM / Lisya Bahar Manoah / Yaniv Shnieder / Active Chairperson / CEO / lisya@elron.com / yaniv@elron.com

**Layout**

Two-column split on a white field: a narrow left text column (about 4% to 31% of width) holds the logo at top, the large title and URL at the vertical middle, and two presenter blocks anchored near the bottom; a large rounded photograph fills the right column from about 33% to 97% width and 5% to 93% height. Left margin is about 4% of slide width and the right margin about 3%. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Logo: the Elron Ventures logo at top left, a blue stylised E-shaped mark of three stacked horizontal bars with rounded right ends, next to the wordmark 'Elron' over 'Ventures' in blue. Photograph: an aerial view of a dense city skyline in Tel Aviv under a blue sky with wispy white cirrus clouds. A tall blue-green glass tower with horizontal balcony bands sits left of centre and a twisted, angled glass skyscraper with a pale blue reflective facade sits right of centre; white and beige mid-rise towers, a white tower with a spire, a grey mesh-clad tower and low urban buildings with red-tiled roofs fill the middle and foreground, with a multi-lane road and traffic in the lower right. Lighting is bright daylight with a warm tone on the buildings. Dominant colours are sky blue, glass teal, white and beige. The image is rectangular with rounded corners, boxed rather than full-bleed, and is not cropped at its visible edges. No overlay or tint. Small blue accent bars: two short thick blue bars with thin blue rules extending right, one under each presenter block, each about 0.5% of slide height thick.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Title 'Thank You!' in a geometric sans-serif such as Inter or Helvetica-like, medium weight, black #000000, about 7.4% of slide height. URL in regular-weight uppercase sans-serif, grey #595959, about 2.7%. Presenter names in medium-weight sans-serif, grey #595959, about 2.2%; roles and emails in regular-weight sans-serif, grey #595959, about 2.0%. Email addresses are underlined. Accent colour is blue #508ECA for the logo, the thick bars and the thin rules. Photo has rounded corners of small radius and no visible shadow.
